log({cue:'tour-start',audioTime:audio.currentTime}); }).catch(function(){ note.textContent='The tour audio could not be played. The essay below works as usual.'; }); } function pauseTour(reason){ if(!playing)return; playing=false;audio.pause(); if(rafId)cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); toggle.textContent='Resume'; statusEl.textContent=(reason==='manual')?'Paused. It scrolls for you':'Tour paused'; btn.textContent='Listen: audio tour'; log({cue:'tour-pause',reason:reason||'button',audioTime:audio.currentTime}); } function resumeTour(){ audio.play().then(function(){ playing=true;toggle.textContent='Pause';statusEl.textContent='Tour playing'; btn.textContent='Tour playing';lastY=window.scrollY;tick(); log({cue:'tour-resume',audioTime:audio.currentTime}); }).catch(function(){}); } function stopTour(){ pauseTour('stop'); audio.currentTime=0;fired={};fill.style.width='0'; bar.classList.remove('on');statusEl.textContent='Tour playing'; log({cue:'tour-stop',audioTime:0}); } btn.addEventListener('click',function(){ if(playing){pauseTour('button');} else if(audio.currentTime>0&&!audio.ended){resumeTour();} else{startTour();} }); toggle.addEventListener('click',function(){ if(playing){pauseTour('button');}else{resumeTour();} }); stopBtn.addEventListener('click',stopTour); audio.addEventListener('ended',function(){ playing=false;if(rafId)cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); bar.classList.remove('on');btn.textContent='Listen: audio tour'; fill.style.width='100%'; log({cue:'tour-ended',audioTime:audio.currentTime}); }); audio.addEventListener('error',function(){ note.textContent='The tour audio could not be loaded. The essay below works as usual.'; log({cue:'tour-audio-error'}); }); window.addEventListener('wheel',function(){ if(playing)pauseTour('manual'); },{passive:true}); window.addEventListener('touchmove',function(){ if(playing)pauseTour('manual'); },{passive:true}); })();
